方法

|  | 名称 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 公共方法 | [Abort](162f8d03-6052-8386-ea1a-8af9d75ca749.htm) | Aborts an acquisition and returns the digitizer to the Idle state. Call this method if the digitizer times out waiting for a trigger. |
| 公共方法 | [AddWaveformProcessing](904692dd-0c3d-923f-fedd-ff35c7e3546b.htm) | Adds one measurement to the list of processing steps that are completed before the measurement. |
| 公共方法 | [AdjustSampleClockRelativeDelay](3c331ca1-de6c-d699-2087-3752c773ab84.htm) | Configures the relative sample clock delay in seconds when using the internal clock. Each time this method is called, the sample clock is delayed by the specified amount of time. |
| 公共方法 | [AutoSetup](93c5ddb8-9625-7179-11f9-e94445d93dfd.htm) | Automatically configures the instrument. When this method is called, the digitizer senses the input signal and automatically configures many of the instrument settings. If a signal is detected on a channel, the driver chooses the smallest available vertical range that is larger than the signal range. |
| 公共方法 | [ClearWaveformMeasurementStats](5aba081a-ce7b-d6b0-83f1-91ac03c939f6.htm) | Clears the waveform statistics on the channel and for all measurements. |
| 公共方法 | [ClearWaveformMeasurementStats(String)](4b3d5e72-ae84-dc76-6789-5b38472d4232.htm) | Clears the waveform statistics on the channel and for scalar measurements. |
| 公共方法 | [ClearWaveformProcessing](f624d93e-6f44-45b0-56f8-ce28fca8823e.htm) | Clears the list of processing steps assigned to the given channel. |
| 公共方法 | [ConfigConsorWithDiffSource](922a2233-76b7-ec2e-4805-8eb95a115754.htm) | 配置异源差分探头 |
| 公共方法 | [ConfigConsorWithSameSource](6618e228-65cf-1b99-9a93-15a2c38b0408.htm) | 配置同源差分探头 |
| 公共方法 | [ConfigureAcquisitionRecord](a593b787-44d1-8b1a-a37f-5d3173e606ed.htm) | Configures the most commonly configured attributes of the oscilloscope acquisition sub-system. These attributes are the time per record, minimum record length, and the acquisition start time. |
| 公共方法 | [ConfigureChanCharacteristics](ef50715b-31b7-d09d-3fe3-2346cd09e8c0.htm) | Configures the attributes that control the electrical characteristics of the channel. These attributes are the input impedance and the maximum frequency of the input signal. |
| 公共方法 | [ConfigureChannel](3864b745-f507-ac65-be6f-9969203e7e22.htm) | Configures the most commonly-configured properties, such as the range, offset, coupling, probe attenuation, and the channel name of the digitizer vertical subsystem. |
| 公共方法 | [ConfigureClock](a3810c31-8cc9-f6fa-d23e-e98a46431b21.htm) | Configures the properties for synchronizing the digitizer to a reference or sending the digitizer's reference clock output to be used as a synchronizing clock for other digitizers. |
| 公共方法 | [ConfigureHorizontalTiming](c0c8aa73-97d3-54e5-bc1c-7c7378288ae1.htm) | Configures the common properties of the horizontal subsystem for a multirecord acquisition in terms of minimum sample rate. |
| 公共方法 | [ConfigureRefLevels](211f3b9b-35db-132f-40dc-3d388e08f883.htm) | Configure the low, mid and high reference level. |
| 公共方法 | [ConfigureTriggerDigital](ed285f96-0c21-c9b7-272c-a32489fa601a.htm) | Configures the common properties of a digital trigger. |
| 公共方法 | [ConfigureTriggerEdge](6d2da512-576a-ab03-90f9-42b25194ac26.htm) | Configures common properties for analog edge triggering. |
| 公共方法 | [ConfigureTriggerHysteresis](aa88a162-cb02-53c4-1a5f-62708cf6dce4.htm) | Configures common properties for analog hysteresis triggering. |
| 公共方法 | [ConfigureTriggerImmediate](e158d8f8-9e16-e465-5567-ff56fb0298e0.htm) | Configures common properties for immediate triggering. Immediate triggering means the digitizer triggers itself. |
| 公共方法 | [ConfigureTriggerSoftware](efca2263-f72a-e1d0-05de-1bb1b9ee5e6a.htm) | Configures common properties for software triggering. |
| 公共方法 | [Disable](e9742719-67c8-f447-e9af-81f279a8fe58.htm) | Places the instrument in a quiescent state as quickly as possible. |
| 公共方法 | Equals | Determines whether the specified object is equal to the current object. (继承自 Object。) |
| 公共方法 | [ExportSignal](af960d06-707e-95f2-1959-1cf1e93cabed.htm) | Configures the digitizer to generate a signal that other instruments can detect when configured for digital triggering or sharing clocks. |
| 公共方法 | [FetchArrayMeasurement](b6759e30-af3f-ec32-7163-c0034d58cd17.htm) | Obtains a waveform from the digitizer and returns the specified measurement array. This method may return multiple waveforms depending on the number of channels, the acquisition type, and the number of records you specify. |
| 公共方法 | [FetchMeasurementStats](bf592dd2-f1b4-7cf9-1e7c-7d7fba9e4d89.htm) | Obtains a waveform measurement and returns the measurement value.This method may return multiple statistical results depending on the number of channels, the acquisition type, and the number of records you specify. |
| 公共方法 | [FetchWaveform](669c1b07-f90b-2f8d-5f25-044709be86ef.htm) | Retrieves data from a previously initiated acquisition. This method may return multiple waveforms depending on the number of channels, the acquisition type, and the number of records you specify. |
| 公共方法 | [FetchWaveformMeasurement](84aada68-5049-19fc-4e14-5e9424faa69f.htm) | Fetches a waveform from the digitizer and performs the specified waveform measurement. This method may return multiple values depending on the number of channels, the acquisition type, and the number of records you specify. |

Configures the relative sample clock delay in seconds when using the internal clock. Each time this method is called, the sample clock is delayed by the specified amount of time.

[复制](# "复制")

```

	double delay
)
```

delay  Double
:   The amount of time, in seconds, to delay the sample clock. This is a relative value, so repeated calls to this method delays the sample clock by this amount every time.

#### AutoSetup 方法

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
|  | ScopeAutoSetup 方法 |

Automatically configures the instrument. When this method is called, the digitizer senses the input signal and automatically configures many of the instrument settings. If a signal is detected on a channel, the driver chooses the smallest available vertical range that is larger than the signal range.

#### ConfigureAcquisitionRecord 方法

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
|  | ScopeConfigureAcquisitionRecord 方法 |

Configures the most commonly configured attributes of the oscilloscope acquisition sub-system. These attributes are the time per record, minimum record length, and the acquisition start time.

  
**命名空间：** [ScopeParent](2e81ae17-972d-cb8b-77cc-a2cc3e51ad2e.htm)  
**程序集：** ScopeMeasStation (在 ScopeMeasStation.dll 中) 版本：2.0.0.0+a7bc1cebb515af7103d79fec111f1a66ff778f8d

语法

C#

[复制](# "复制")

```
public Scope ConfigureAcquisitionRecord(
	double timePerRecord,
	long minimumNumberPoints,
	double aquisitionStartTime
)
```

###### 参数

timePerRecord  Double
:   The length of time that corresponds to the record length, in seconds.

minimumNumberPoints  Int64
:   The minimum number of points you require in the waveform record for each channel.

aquisitionStartTime  Double
:   The length of time, in seconds, from the trigger event to the first point in the waveform record.

###### 返回值

[Scope](9bb822ec-b57c-5075-7b11-c53eb488fd08.htm)  
Return ScopeParent.Scope instance.

Configures the most commonly-configured properties, such as the range, offset, coupling, probe attenuation, and the channel name of the digitizer vertical subsystem.

  
**命名空间：** [ScopeParent](2e81ae17-972d-cb8b-77cc-a2cc3e51ad2e.htm)

[复制](# "复制")

```

	double range,
	double offset,
	string coupling,
	double probeAttenuation,
	bool enabled
)
```

###### 参数

range  Double
:   The absolute value, in volts, of the input range for a channel.

offset  Double
:   The location of the center of the range with respect to ground in volts.

coupling  String
:   Valid values are "AC", "DC" and "Ground".

probeAttenuation  Double
:   The probe attenuation for the input channel.

enabled  Boolean

#### ConfigureClock 方法

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
|  | ScopeConfigureClock 方法 |

Configures the properties for synchronizing the digitizer to a reference or sending the digitizer's reference clock output to be used as a synchronizing clock for other digitizers.

  
**命名空间：** [ScopeParent](2e81ae17-972d-cb8b-77cc-a2cc3e51ad2e.htm)  
**程序集：** ScopeMeasStation (在 ScopeMeasStation.dll 中) 版本：2.0.0.0+a7bc1cebb515af7103d79fec111f1a66ff778f8d

语法

C#

[复制](# "复制")

```
public Scope ConfigureClock(
	string inputClockSource,
	string outputClockSource,
	string clockSynchronizationPulseSource,
	bool masterEnabled
)
```

###### 参数

inputClockSource  String
:   The input source for the reference clock to which the 100 MHz sample clock is phase-locked. Possible values are
    "RtsiClock",
    "PxiClock",
    "NoSource",
    "External",
    "ClockIn",
    "Aux0ClkIn",
    "Pfi0",
    "Pfi1",
    "Pfi2".

outputClockSource  String
:   The output source for the reference clock to which another scope's sample clock can be phased-locked. Possible values are
    "RtsiClock",
    "NoSource",
    "ClockOut",
    "Aux0ClkOut",
    "Pfi0",
    "Pfi1",
    "Pfi2",
    "Rtsi0",
    "Rtsi1",
    "Rtsi2",
    "Rtsi3",
    "Rtsi4",
    "Rtsi5",
    "Rtsi6",
    "Rtsi7".

clockSynchronizationPulseSource  String
:   Possible values are
    "NoSource",
    "Pfi0",
    "Pfi1",
    "Pfi2",
    "Rtsi0",
    "Rtsi1",
    "Rtsi2",
    "Rtsi3",
    "Rtsi4",
    "Rtsi5",
    "Rtsi6",
    "Rtsi7".

masterEnabled  Boolean
:   Specifies whether you want the instrument to be a master or a slave. The master instrument is typically the originator of the trigger signal and clock sync pulse. For a standalone instrument, set this property to false.

###### 返回值

[Scope](9bb822ec-b57c-5075-7b11-c53eb488fd08.htm)  
Return ScopeParent.Scope instance.

参见

###### 引用

[Scope 类](9bb822ec-b57c-5075-7b11-c53eb488fd08.htm)

[ScopeParent 命名空间](2e81ae17-972d-cb8b-77cc-a2cc3e51ad2e.htm)

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
|  | ScopeConfigureHorizontalTiming 方法 |

Configures the common properties of the horizontal subsystem for a multirecord acquisition in terms of minimum sample rate.

  
**命名空间：** [ScopeParent](2e81ae17-972d-cb8b-77cc-a2cc3e51ad2e.htm)  
**程序集：** ScopeMeasStation (在 ScopeMeasStation.dll 中) 版本：2.0.0.0+a7bc1cebb515af7103d79fec111f1a66ff778f8d

C#

[复制](# "复制")

```
public Scope ConfigureHorizontalTiming(
	double sampleRateMin,
	long numberOfPointsMin,
	double referencePosition,
	long numberOfRecords,
	bool enforceRealtime
)
```

###### 参数

sampleRateMin  Double
:   The sampling rate for the acquisition.

numberOfPointsMin  Int64
:   The minimum number of points you need in the record for each channel

referencePosition  Double
:   The position of the reference event in the waveform record specified as a percentage.

numberOfRecords  Int64
:   The number of records to acquire.

enforceRealtime  Boolean
:   Indicates whether the digitizer enforces real-time measurements or allows equivalent-time (RIS) measurements.

#### ConfigureTriggerDigital 方法

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
|  | ScopeConfigureTriggerDigital 方法 |

Configures the common properties of a digital trigger.

  
**命名空间：** [ScopeParent](2e81ae17-972d-cb8b-77cc-a2cc3e51ad2e.htm)  
**程序集：** ScopeMeasStation (在 ScopeMeasStation.dll 中) 版本：2.0.0.0+a7bc1cebb515af7103d79fec111f1a66ff778f8d

语法

C#

[复制](# "复制")

```
public Scope ConfigureTriggerDigital(
	string triggerSource,
	string triggerSlope,
	double triggerHoldoff,
	double triggerDelay
)
```

###### 参数

triggerSource  String
:   The trigger source. Refer to the instrument specifications , possibe values are list below.
    "Aux0Pfi0",
    "Aux0Pfi1",
    "Aux0Pfi2",
    "Aux0Pfi3",
    "Aux0Pfi4",
    "Aux0Pfi5",
    "Aux0Pfi6",
    "Aux0Pfi7",
    "External",
    "Pfi0",
    "Pfi1",
    "Pfi2",
    "Rtsi0",
    "Rtsi1",
    "Rtsi2",
    "Rtsi3",
    "Rtsi4",
    "Rtsi5",
    "Rtsi6",
    "Rtsi7",
    "PxiStar",
    "Immediate",
    "SoftwareTriggerFunction".

triggerSlope  String
:   A value indicating whether you want a rising edge or a falling edge to trigger the digitizer. Valid values are "Negative" and "Positive".

triggerHoldoff  Double
:   The length of time, in seconds, that the digitizer waits after detecting a trigger before enable to detect another trigger.

triggerDelay  Double
:   The length of time, in seconds, that the digitizer waits after receiving the trigger to start acquiring data.

triggerHoldoff  Double
:   The length of time the digitizer waits after detecting a trigger before enabe to detect another trigger.

triggerDelay  Double
:   The length of time the digitizer waits after receiving the trigger to start acquiring data.

#### FetchArrayMeasurement 方法

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
|  | ScopeFetchArrayMeasurement 方法 |

Obtains a waveform from the digitizer and returns the specified measurement array. This method may return multiple waveforms depending on the number of channels, the acquisition type, and the number of records you specify.

  
**命名空间：** [ScopeParent](2e81ae17-972d-cb8b-77cc-a2cc3e51ad2e.htm)  
**程序集：** ScopeMeasStation (在 ScopeMeasStation.dll 中) 版本：2.0.0.0+a7bc1cebb515af7103d79fec111f1a66ff778f8d

语法

C#

[复制](# "复制")

```
public Dictionary<string, double[]> FetchArrayMeasurement(
	double timeout,
	string arrayMeasurementType
)
```

###### 参数

timeout  Double
:   The time to wait for data to be acquired. Use 0 for this parameter to fetch whatever is currently available. Using -1 for this parameter implies infinite timeout.

arrayMeasurementType  String
:   The type of the array measurement to perform. Valid values are
    "NoMeasurement",
    "LastAcquisitionHistogram",
    "FftPhaseSpectrum",
    "FftAmplitudeSpectrumVoltsRms",
    "MultipleAcquisitionVoltageHistogram",
    "MultipleAcquisitionTimeHistogram",
    "ArrayIntegral",
    "Derivative",
    "Inverse",
    "HanningWindow",
    "FlatTopWindow",
    "PolynomialInterpolation",
    "MultiplyChannels",
    "AddChannels",
    "SubtractChannels",
    "DivideChannels",
    "MultipleAcquisitionAverage",
    "ButterworthFilter",
    "ChebyshevFilter",
    "FftAmplitudeSpectrumDB",
    "HammingWindow",
    "WindowedFirFilter",
    "BesselFilter",
    "TriangleWindow",
    "BlackmanWindow",
    "ArrayOffset",
    "ArrayGain".

###### 返回值

DictionaryString, Double  
Key: single pin name.
Value: waveform sample array, total count = recordLength \* numberOfRecords, order by record index.
Such as recordLength = 1000 numberOfRecords = 2,
0 ~ 999 in the value is the fist record data,
1000 ~ 1999 is the second record data.

参见

###### 引用

[Scope 类](9bb822ec-b57c-5075-7b11-c53eb488fd08.htm)

[ScopeParent 命名空间](2e81ae17-972d-cb8b-77cc-a2cc3e51ad2e.htm)

#### ReadWaveform 方法

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
|  | ScopeReadWaveform 方法 |

Initiates an acquisition, waits for it to complete, and retrieves the data.This method may return multiple waveforms depending on the number of channels, the acquisition type, and the number of records you specify.

  
**命名空间：** [ScopeParent](2e81ae17-972d-cb8b-77cc-a2cc3e51ad2e.htm)  
**程序集：** ScopeMeasStation (在 ScopeMeasStation.dll 中) 版本：2.0.0.0+a7bc1cebb515af7103d79fec111f1a66ff778f8d

语法

C#

[复制](# "复制")

```
public Dictionary<string, double[]> ReadWaveform(
	double timeout,
	long numberOfSamples
)
```

###### 参数

timeout  Double
:   The time to wait for data to be acquired. Use 0 for this parameter to fetch whatever is currently available. Using -1 for this parameter implies infinite timeout.

numberOfSamples  Int64
:   The maximum number of samples to fetch for each waveform. Use –1 for this parameter if you want to fetch all available samples.

###### 返回值

DictionaryString, Double  
Key: single pin name.
Value: waveform sample array, total count = recordLength \* numberOfRecords, order by record index.
such as recordLength = 1000 numberOfRecords = 2,
0 ~ 999 in the value is the fist record data,
1000 ~ 1999 is the second record data.

参见

###### 引用

[Scope 类](9bb822ec-b57c-5075-7b11-c53eb488fd08.htm)

[ScopeParent 命名空间](2e81ae17-972d-cb8b-77cc-a2cc3e51ad2e.htm)

    "FftFrequency",
    "FftAmplitude",
    "RiseSlewRate",

    "ACEstimate",
    "DCEstimate",
    "TimeDelay",
    "AveragePeriod",

    "VoltageBaseToTop",

    "VoltageHistogramMean",

    "VoltageHistogramPeakToPeak",
    "VoltageHistogramMedian",
    "VoltageHistogramHits",
    "VoltageHistogramMax",
    "VoltageHistogramMin",
    "VoltageHistogramMeanPlusStandardDeviation",
    "VoltageHistogramMeanPlus2StandardDeviation",

    "VoltageHistogramNewHits",
    "TimeHistogramMean",
